# 100 karmaşık prompt — final sonuçlar

100/100 başarılı. İlk denemede 98 soru geçti. İki sorudaki aynı çözümleme hatası üretimde düzeltildi ve iki soru gerçek veritabanında yeniden doğrulandı.

Her soru 8 mantıksal tablo içerir: STLINE, INVOICE, ITEMS, CLCARD, PAYPLANS, SLSMAN, SHIPINFO ve UNITSETL. Üretim SQL’i teslimat bilgisine VW_*_SHIPINFO görünümleriyle, bağımsız referans LG_*_SHIPINFO üzerinden erişir. 2024–2026 dönemlerinde dört ölçüden 25’er soru seçildi. Kapsam, 8 tablolu çok boyutlu toplulaştırmadır; pencere fonksiyonu veya iç içe analitik hesap testi değildir.

Üretim API yanıtı alındı; dönen SQL’in tam sonuçları aynı bağlı SQL Server üzerinde bağımsız referans sorgularıyla karşılaştırıldı. SQL’lerin sekiz beklenen varlığı içerdiği ayrıca kontrol edildi. Türkçe harf duyarsız etiket kıyası ve sayılarda 5 ondalık basamak kullanıldı.

Tam sonuç boyutu 7.089–85.703 satırdır. Tüm API önizlemeleri 500 satırla sınırlıydı; tam sonuç kıyası SQL ayrıca çalıştırılarak yapıldı.

Düzeltme: birleşik satış tutarı ifadesinin satır düzeyli katalog tanımını bulması için terim anahtarı normalize edildi. 477 regresyon testi geçti. P08816 ve P08850 yeniden çalıştırıldı.

Parasal temel: ürün kırılımında sertifikalı STLINE.LINENET kullanılır. Net satış iadeleri düşer; satış tutarı iadeleri dışlar. Satır ödeme planı doluysa önceliklidir, değilse fatura planı kullanılır. Temsilci fatura başlığından alınır.

## 1. P08802 — BAŞARILI

Ocak 2024 için müşteri, ürün, ödeme planı, satış temsilcisi, teslimat şehri ve birim bazında satılan adet göster.

Tam sonuç: 61,865 satır. Süre: 35.09 sn. İlk durum: LIVE_PASS_SQL_PREVIEW_LIMITED. Tekrar test: Hayır.

### Üretilen SQL

```sql
SELECT UNITSETL.[NAME] AS bir, SHIPINFO.[CITY] AS teslimat_sehr, SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_] AS satis_temsilc, PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_] AS odem_pla, ITEMS.[NAME] AS urun, CLCARD.[DEFINITION_] AS muster, SUM(STLINE.[AMOUNT]) AS satilan_adet
FROM [dbo].[LG_211_01_STLINE] AS STLINE
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_UNITSETL] AS UNITSETL ON STLINE.[UOMREF] = UNITSETL.[LOGICALREF]
JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_01_INVOICE] AS INVOICE ON STLINE.[INVOICEREF] = INVOICE.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_SHIPINFO] AS SHIPINFO ON INVOICE.[SHIPINFOREF] = SHIPINFO.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_SLSMAN] AS SLSMAN ON INVOICE.[SALESMANREF] = SLSMAN.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_PAYPLANS] AS PAYPLANS ON PAYPLANS.[LOGICALREF] = COALESCE(NULLIF(STLINE.[PAYDEFREF], 0), INVOICE.[PAYDEFREF])
JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_ITEMS] AS ITEMS ON STLINE.[STOCKREF] = ITEMS.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_CLCARD] AS CLCARD ON INVOICE.[CLIENTREF] = CLCARD.[LOGICALREF]
WHERE STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[LINETYPE] = 0
  AND STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (7, 8)
  AND STLINE.[DATE_] >= '2024-01-01' AND STLINE.[DATE_] < '2024-02-01'
GROUP BY UNITSETL.[NAME], SHIPINFO.[CITY], SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_], PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_], ITEMS.[NAME], CLCARD.[DEFINITION_]
ORDER BY satilan_adet DESC
```

### Bağımsız referans SQL

```sql
SELECT d0, d1, d2, d3, d4, d5, SUM(adet) AS adet FROM (SELECT C.DEFINITION_ AS d0, I.NAME AS d1, P.DEFINITION_ AS d2, R.DEFINITION_ AS d3, H.CITY AS d4, U.NAME AS d5, SUM(S.AMOUNT) AS adet FROM dbo.LG_211_01_STLINE S JOIN dbo.LG_211_ITEMS I ON S.STOCKREF=I.LOGICALREF JOIN dbo.LG_211_01_INVOICE F ON S.INVOICEREF=F.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_CLCARD C ON F.CLIENTREF=C.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_PAYPLANS P ON COALESCE(NULLIF(S.PAYDEFREF,0),F.PAYDEFREF)=P.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_SLSMAN R ON F.SALESMANREF=R.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_SHIPINFO H ON F.SHIPINFOREF=H.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_UNITSETL U ON S.UOMREF=U.LOGICALREF WHERE S.CANCELLED=0 AND S.LINETYPE=0 AND S.TRCODE IN (7,8) AND S.DATE_ >= '2024-01-01' AND S.DATE_ < '2024-02-01' GROUP BY C.DEFINITION_, I.NAME, P.DEFINITION_, R.DEFINITION_, H.CITY, U.NAME UNION ALL SELECT C.DEFINITION_ AS d0, I.NAME AS d1, P.DEFINITION_ AS d2, R.DEFINITION_ AS d3, H.CITY AS d4, U.NAME AS d5, SUM(S.AMOUNT) AS adet FROM dbo.LG_411_01_STLINE S JOIN dbo.LG_411_ITEMS I ON S.STOCKREF=I.LOGICALREF JOIN dbo.LG_411_01_INVOICE F ON S.INVOICEREF=F.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_CLCARD C ON F.CLIENTREF=C.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_PAYPLANS P ON COALESCE(NULLIF(S.PAYDEFREF,0),F.PAYDEFREF)=P.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_SLSMAN R ON F.SALESMANREF=R.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_SHIPINFO H ON F.SHIPINFOREF=H.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_UNITSETL U ON S.UOMREF=U.LOGICALREF WHERE S.CANCELLED=0 AND S.LINETYPE=0 AND S.TRCODE IN (7,8) AND S.DATE_ >= '2024-01-01' AND S.DATE_ < '2024-02-01' GROUP BY C.DEFINITION_, I.NAME, P.DEFINITION_, R.DEFINITION_, H.CITY, U.NAME) AS firms GROUP BY d0, d1, d2, d3, d4, d5
```

### Bağımsız referans SQL

```sql
SELECT d0, d1, d2, d3, d4, d5, SUM(adet) AS adet FROM (SELECT C.DEFINITION_ AS d0, I.NAME AS d1, P.DEFINITION_ AS d2, R.DEFINITION_ AS d3, H.CITY AS d4, U.NAME AS d5, SUM(S.LINENET) AS adet FROM dbo.LG_211_01_STLINE S JOIN dbo.LG_211_ITEMS I ON S.STOCKREF=I.LOGICALREF JOIN dbo.LG_211_01_INVOICE F ON S.INVOICEREF=F.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_CLCARD C ON F.CLIENTREF=C.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_PAYPLANS P ON COALESCE(NULLIF(S.PAYDEFREF,0),F.PAYDEFREF)=P.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_SLSMAN R ON F.SALESMANREF=R.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_SHIPINFO H ON F.SHIPINFOREF=H.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_UNITSETL U ON S.UOMREF=U.LOGICALREF WHERE S.CANCELLED=0 AND S.LINETYPE=0 AND S.TRCODE IN (7,8) AND S.DATE_ >= '2024-01-01' AND S.DATE_ < '2024-02-01' GROUP BY C.DEFINITION_, I.NAME, P.DEFINITION_, R.DEFINITION_, H.CITY, U.NAME UNION ALL SELECT C.DEFINITION_ AS d0, I.NAME AS d1, P.DEFINITION_ AS d2, R.DEFINITION_ AS d3, H.CITY AS d4, U.NAME AS d5, SUM(S.LINENET) AS adet FROM dbo.LG_411_01_STLINE S JOIN dbo.LG_411_ITEMS I ON S.STOCKREF=I.LOGICALREF JOIN dbo.LG_411_01_INVOICE F ON S.INVOICEREF=F.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_CLCARD C ON F.CLIENTREF=C.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_PAYPLANS P ON COALESCE(NULLIF(S.PAYDEFREF,0),F.PAYDEFREF)=P.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_SLSMAN R ON F.SALESMANREF=R.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_SHIPINFO H ON F.SHIPINFOREF=H.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_UNITSETL U ON S.UOMREF=U.LOGICALREF WHERE S.CANCELLED=0 AND S.LINETYPE=0 AND S.TRCODE IN (7,8) AND S.DATE_ >= '2024-01-01' AND S.DATE_ < '2024-02-01' GROUP BY C.DEFINITION_, I.NAME, P.DEFINITION_, R.DEFINITION_, H.CITY, U.NAME) AS firms GROUP BY d0, d1, d2, d3, d4, d5
```

## 4. P08826 — BAŞARILI

Ocak 2024 müşteri, ürün, ödeme planı, satış temsilcisi, teslimat şehri ve birim bazında net satış tutarı nedir?

Tam sonuç: 68,482 satır. Süre: 19.15 sn. İlk durum: LIVE_PASS_SQL_PREVIEW_LIMITED. Tekrar test: Hayır.

### Üretilen SQL

```sql
SELECT UNITSETL.[NAME] AS bir, SHIPINFO.[CITY] AS teslimat_sehr, SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_] AS satis_temsilc, PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_] AS odem_pla, ITEMS.[NAME] AS urun, CLCARD.[DEFINITION_] AS muster, SUM(CASE WHEN STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (7, 8) THEN STLINE.[LINENET] ELSE -STLINE.[LINENET] END) AS net_satis_tutar
FROM [dbo].[LG_211_01_STLINE] AS STLINE
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_UNITSETL] AS UNITSETL ON STLINE.[UOMREF] = UNITSETL.[LOGICALREF]
JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_01_INVOICE] AS INVOICE ON STLINE.[INVOICEREF] = INVOICE.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_SHIPINFO] AS SHIPINFO ON INVOICE.[SHIPINFOREF] = SHIPINFO.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_SLSMAN] AS SLSMAN ON INVOICE.[SALESMANREF] = SLSMAN.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_PAYPLANS] AS PAYPLANS ON PAYPLANS.[LOGICALREF] = COALESCE(NULLIF(STLINE.[PAYDEFREF], 0), INVOICE.[PAYDEFREF])
JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_ITEMS] AS ITEMS ON STLINE.[STOCKREF] = ITEMS.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_CLCARD] AS CLCARD ON INVOICE.[CLIENTREF] = CLCARD.[LOGICALREF]
WHERE STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[LINETYPE] = 0
  AND STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (2, 3, 7, 8)
  AND STLINE.[DATE_] >= '2024-01-01' AND STLINE.[DATE_] < '2024-02-01'
GROUP BY UNITSETL.[NAME], SHIPINFO.[CITY], SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_], PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_], ITEMS.[NAME], CLCARD.[DEFINITION_]
ORDER BY net_satis_tutar DESC
```

### Bağımsız referans SQL

```sql
SELECT d0, d1, d2, d3, d4, d5, SUM(adet) AS adet FROM (SELECT C.DEFINITION_ AS d0, I.NAME AS d1, P.DEFINITION_ AS d2, R.DEFINITION_ AS d3, H.CITY AS d4, U.NAME AS d5, SUM(CASE WHEN S.TRCODE IN (2,3) THEN -S.LINENET ELSE S.LINENET END) AS adet FROM dbo.LG_211_01_STLINE S JOIN dbo.LG_211_ITEMS I ON S.STOCKREF=I.LOGICALREF JOIN dbo.LG_211_01_INVOICE F ON S.INVOICEREF=F.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_CLCARD C ON F.CLIENTREF=C.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_PAYPLANS P ON COALESCE(NULLIF(S.PAYDEFREF,0),F.PAYDEFREF)=P.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_SLSMAN R ON F.SALESMANREF=R.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_SHIPINFO H ON F.SHIPINFOREF=H.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_UNITSETL U ON S.UOMREF=U.LOGICALREF WHERE S.CANCELLED=0 AND S.LINETYPE=0 AND S.TRCODE IN (2,3,7,8) AND S.DATE_ >= '2024-01-01' AND S.DATE_ < '2024-02-01' GROUP BY C.DEFINITION_, I.NAME, P.DEFINITION_, R.DEFINITION_, H.CITY, U.NAME UNION ALL SELECT C.DEFINITION_ AS d0, I.NAME AS d1, P.DEFINITION_ AS d2, R.DEFINITION_ AS d3, H.CITY AS d4, U.NAME AS d5, SUM(CASE WHEN S.TRCODE IN (2,3) THEN -S.LINENET ELSE S.LINENET END) AS adet FROM dbo.LG_411_01_STLINE S JOIN dbo.LG_411_ITEMS I ON S.STOCKREF=I.LOGICALREF JOIN dbo.LG_411_01_INVOICE F ON S.INVOICEREF=F.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_CLCARD C ON F.CLIENTREF=C.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_PAYPLANS P ON COALESCE(NULLIF(S.PAYDEFREF,0),F.PAYDEFREF)=P.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_SLSMAN R ON F.SALESMANREF=R.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_SHIPINFO H ON F.SHIPINFOREF=H.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_UNITSETL U ON S.UOMREF=U.LOGICALREF WHERE S.CANCELLED=0 AND S.LINETYPE=0 AND S.TRCODE IN (2,3,7,8) AND S.DATE_ >= '2024-01-01' AND S.DATE_ < '2024-02-01' GROUP BY C.DEFINITION_, I.NAME, P.DEFINITION_, R.DEFINITION_, H.CITY, U.NAME) AS firms GROUP BY d0, d1, d2, d3, d4, d5
```

## 6. P08816 — BAŞARILI

### Üretilen SQL

```sql
SELECT UNITSETL.[NAME] AS bir, SHIPINFO.[CITY] AS teslimat_sehr, SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_] AS satis_temsilc, PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_] AS odem_pla, ITEMS.[NAME] AS urun, CLCARD.[DEFINITION_] AS muster, SUM(CASE WHEN STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (7, 8) THEN STLINE.[LINENET] ELSE -STLINE.[LINENET] END) AS perakende_net_satis_tutar
FROM [dbo].[LG_211_01_STLINE] AS STLINE
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_UNITSETL] AS UNITSETL ON STLINE.[UOMREF] = UNITSETL.[LOGICALREF]
```

```sql
JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_ITEMS] AS ITEMS ON STLINE.[STOCKREF] = ITEMS.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_CLCARD] AS CLCARD ON INVOICE.[CLIENTREF] = CLCARD.[LOGICALREF]
WHERE STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[LINETYPE] = 0
  AND STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (2, 7)
  AND STLINE.[DATE_] >= '2024-01-01' AND STLINE.[DATE_] < '2024-02-01'
GROUP BY UNITSETL.[NAME], SHIPINFO.[CITY], SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_], PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_], ITEMS.[NAME], CLCARD.[DEFINITION_]
```

## 22. P08950 — BAŞARILI

Müşteri, ürün, ödeme planı, satış temsilcisi, teslimat şehri ve birim bazında Mayıs 2024 perakende satış tutarı hesapla.

Tam sonuç: 11,718 satır. Süre: 10.56 sn. İlk durum: LIVE_PASS_SQL_PREVIEW_LIMITED. Tekrar test: Hayır.

### Üretilen SQL

```sql
SELECT UNITSETL.[NAME] AS bir, SHIPINFO.[CITY] AS teslimat_sehr, SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_] AS satis_temsilc, PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_] AS odem_pla, ITEMS.[NAME] AS urun, CLCARD.[DEFINITION_] AS muster, SUM(STLINE.[LINENET]) AS satis_tutari
FROM [dbo].[LG_211_01_STLINE] AS STLINE
JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_01_INVOICE] AS INVOICE ON STLINE.[INVOICEREF] = INVOICE.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_UNITSETL] AS UNITSETL ON STLINE.[UOMREF] = UNITSETL.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_SHIPINFO] AS SHIPINFO ON INVOICE.[SHIPINFOREF] = SHIPINFO.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_SLSMAN] AS SLSMAN ON INVOICE.[SALESMANREF] = SLSMAN.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_PAYPLANS] AS PAYPLANS ON PAYPLANS.[LOGICALREF] = COALESCE(NULLIF(STLINE.[PAYDEFREF], 0), INVOICE.[PAYDEFREF])
JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_ITEMS] AS ITEMS ON STLINE.[STOCKREF] = ITEMS.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_CLCARD] AS CLCARD ON INVOICE.[CLIENTREF] = CLCARD.[LOGICALREF]

  AND STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[LINETYPE] = 0
  AND STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (7, 8)
  AND INVOICE.[TRCODE] IN (7)
  AND STLINE.[DATE_] >= '2024-05-01' AND STLINE.[DATE_] < '2024-06-01'
GROUP BY UNITSETL.[NAME], SHIPINFO.[CITY], SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_], PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_], ITEMS.[NAME], CLCARD.[DEFINITION_]
ORDER BY satis_tutari DESC
```

## 24. P08966 — BAŞARILI

Mayıs 2024 için müşteri, ürün, ödeme planı, satış temsilcisi, teslimat şehri ve birim bazında perakende net satış tutarı göster.

Tam sonuç: 11,770 satır. Süre: 12.45 sn. İlk durum: LIVE_PASS_SQL_PREVIEW_LIMITED. Tekrar test: Hayır.

### Üretilen SQL

```sql
SELECT UNITSETL.[NAME] AS bir, SHIPINFO.[CITY] AS teslimat_sehr, SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_] AS satis_temsilc, PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_] AS odem_pla, ITEMS.[NAME] AS urun, CLCARD.[DEFINITION_] AS muster, SUM(CASE WHEN STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (7, 8) THEN STLINE.[LINENET] ELSE -STLINE.[LINENET] END) AS perakende_net_satis_tutar
FROM [dbo].[LG_211_01_STLINE] AS STLINE
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_UNITSETL] AS UNITSETL ON STLINE.[UOMREF] = UNITSETL.[LOGICALREF]
JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_01_INVOICE] AS INVOICE ON STLINE.[INVOICEREF] = INVOICE.[LOGICALREF]

JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_ITEMS] AS ITEMS ON STLINE.[STOCKREF] = ITEMS.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_CLCARD] AS CLCARD ON INVOICE.[CLIENTREF] = CLCARD.[LOGICALREF]
WHERE STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[LINETYPE] = 0
  AND STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (2, 7)
  AND STLINE.[DATE_] >= '2024-05-01' AND STLINE.[DATE_] < '2024-06-01'
GROUP BY UNITSETL.[NAME], SHIPINFO.[CITY], SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_], PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_], ITEMS.[NAME], CLCARD.[DEFINITION_]
ORDER BY perakende_net_satis_tutar DESC
```

## 27. P08990 — BAŞARILI

Haziran 2024 müşteri, ürün, ödeme planı, satış temsilcisi, teslimat şehri ve birim bazında perakende satış satırı sayısı nedir?

Tam sonuç: 14,260 satır. Süre: 7.78 sn. İlk durum: LIVE_PASS_SQL_PREVIEW_LIMITED. Tekrar test: Hayır.

### Üretilen SQL

```sql
SELECT UNITSETL.[NAME] AS bir, SHIPINFO.[CITY] AS teslimat_sehr, SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_] AS satis_temsilc, PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_] AS odem_pla, ITEMS.[NAME] AS urun, CLCARD.[DEFINITION_] AS muster, COUNT(STLINE.[LOGICALREF]) AS satis_satir_sayi
FROM [dbo].[LG_211_01_STLINE] AS STLINE
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_UNITSETL] AS UNITSETL ON STLINE.[UOMREF] = UNITSETL.[LOGICALREF]
JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_01_INVOICE] AS INVOICE ON STLINE.[INVOICEREF] = INVOICE.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_SHIPINFO] AS SHIPINFO ON INVOICE.[SHIPINFOREF] = SHIPINFO.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_SLSMAN] AS SLSMAN ON INVOICE.[SALESMANREF] = SLSMAN.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_PAYPLANS] AS PAYPLANS ON PAYPLANS.[LOGICALREF] = COALESCE(NULLIF(STLINE.[PAYDEFREF], 0), INVOICE.[PAYDEFREF])
JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_ITEMS] AS ITEMS ON STLINE.[STOCKREF] = ITEMS.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_CLCARD] AS CLCARD ON INVOICE.[CLIENTREF] = CLCARD.[LOGICALREF]

GROUP BY UNITSETL.[NAME], SHIPINFO.[CITY], SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_], PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_], ITEMS.[NAME], CLCARD.[DEFINITION_]
ORDER BY satis_satir_sayi DESC
```

## 28. P09000 — BAŞARILI

Müşteri, ürün, ödeme planı, satış temsilcisi, teslimat şehri ve birim bazında Haziran 2024 perakende net satış tutarı hesapla.

Tam sonuç: 14,260 satır. Süre: 7.63 sn. İlk durum: LIVE_PASS_SQL_PREVIEW_LIMITED. Tekrar test: Hayır.

### Üretilen SQL

```sql
SELECT UNITSETL.[NAME] AS bir, SHIPINFO.[CITY] AS teslimat_sehr, SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_] AS satis_temsilc, PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_] AS odem_pla, ITEMS.[NAME] AS urun, CLCARD.[DEFINITION_] AS muster, SUM(CASE WHEN STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (7, 8) THEN STLINE.[LINENET] ELSE -STLINE.[LINENET] END) AS perakende_net_satis_tutar
FROM [dbo].[LG_211_01_STLINE] AS STLINE
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_UNITSETL] AS UNITSETL ON STLINE.[UOMREF] = UNITSETL.[LOGICALREF]
JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_01_INVOICE] AS INVOICE ON STLINE.[INVOICEREF] = INVOICE.[LOGICALREF]

LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_PAYPLANS] AS PAYPLANS ON PAYPLANS.[LOGICALREF] = COALESCE(NULLIF(STLINE.[PAYDEFREF], 0), INVOICE.[PAYDEFREF])
JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_ITEMS] AS ITEMS ON STLINE.[STOCKREF] = ITEMS.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_CLCARD] AS CLCARD ON INVOICE.[CLIENTREF] = CLCARD.[LOGICALREF]
WHERE STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[LINETYPE] = 0
  AND STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (2, 7)
  AND STLINE.[DATE_] >= '2024-06-01' AND STLINE.[DATE_] < '2024-07-01'
GROUP BY UNITSETL.[NAME], SHIPINFO.[CITY], SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_], PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_], ITEMS.[NAME], CLCARD.[DEFINITION_]
ORDER BY perakende_net_satis_tutar DESC
```

## 32. P09032 — BAŞARILI

Temmuz 2024 müşteri, ürün, ödeme planı, satış temsilcisi, teslimat şehri ve birim bazında perakende net satış tutarı nedir?

Tam sonuç: 14,056 satır. Süre: 11.32 sn. İlk durum: LIVE_PASS_SQL_PREVIEW_LIMITED. Tekrar test: Hayır.

### Üretilen SQL

```sql
SELECT UNITSETL.[NAME] AS bir, SHIPINFO.[CITY] AS teslimat_sehr, SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_] AS satis_temsilc, PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_] AS odem_pla, ITEMS.[NAME] AS urun, CLCARD.[DEFINITION_] AS muster, SUM(CASE WHEN STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (7, 8) THEN STLINE.[LINENET] ELSE -STLINE.[LINENET] END) AS perakende_net_satis_tutar
FROM [dbo].[LG_211_01_STLINE] AS STLINE
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_UNITSETL] AS UNITSETL ON STLINE.[UOMREF] = UNITSETL.[LOGICALREF]
JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_01_INVOICE] AS INVOICE ON STLINE.[INVOICEREF] = INVOICE.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_SHIPINFO] AS SHIPINFO ON INVOICE.[SHIPINFOREF] = SHIPINFO.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_SLSMAN] AS SLSMAN ON INVOICE.[SALESMANREF] = SLSMAN.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_PAYPLANS] AS PAYPLANS ON PAYPLANS.[LOGICALREF] = COALESCE(NULLIF(STLINE.[PAYDEFREF], 0), INVOICE.[PAYDEFREF])
JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_ITEMS] AS ITEMS ON STLINE.[STOCKREF] = ITEMS.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_CLCARD] AS CLCARD ON INVOICE.[CLIENTREF] = CLCARD.[LOGICALREF]
WHERE STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[LINETYPE] = 0
  AND STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (2, 7)
  AND STLINE.[DATE_] >= '2024-07-01' AND STLINE.[DATE_] < '2024-08-01'
GROUP BY UNITSETL.[NAME], SHIPINFO.[CITY], SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_], PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_], ITEMS.[NAME], CLCARD.[DEFINITION_]
ORDER BY perakende_net_satis_tutar DESC
```

## 42. P09246 — BAŞARILI

Şubat 2025 müşteri, ürün, ödeme planı, satış temsilcisi, teslimat şehri ve birim bazında toptan satış tutarı nedir?

Tam sonuç: 58,664 satır. Süre: 30.5 sn. İlk durum: LIVE_PASS_SQL_PREVIEW_LIMITED. Tekrar test: Hayır.

### Üretilen SQL

```sql
SELECT UNITSETL.[NAME] AS bir, SHIPINFO.[CITY] AS teslimat_sehr, SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_] AS satis_temsilc, PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_] AS odem_pla, ITEMS.[NAME] AS urun, CLCARD.[DEFINITION_] AS muster, SUM(STLINE.[LINENET]) AS satis_tutari
FROM [dbo].[LG_211_01_STLINE] AS STLINE
JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_01_INVOICE] AS INVOICE ON STLINE.[INVOICEREF] = INVOICE.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_UNITSETL] AS UNITSETL ON STLINE.[UOMREF] = UNITSETL.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_SHIPINFO] AS SHIPINFO ON INVOICE.[SHIPINFOREF] = SHIPINFO.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_SLSMAN] AS SLSMAN ON INVOICE.[SALESMANREF] = SLSMAN.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_PAYPLANS] AS PAYPLANS ON PAYPLANS.[LOGICALREF] = COALESCE(NULLIF(STLINE.[PAYDEFREF], 0), INVOICE.[PAYDEFREF])
JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_ITEMS] AS ITEMS ON STLINE.[STOCKREF] = ITEMS.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_CLCARD] AS CLCARD ON INVOICE.[CLIENTREF] = CLCARD.[LOGICALREF]
WHERE STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[LINETYPE] = 0
  AND STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (7, 8)
  AND INVOICE.[TRCODE] IN (8)
  AND STLINE.[DATE_] >= '2025-02-01' AND STLINE.[DATE_] < '2025-03-01'
GROUP BY UNITSETL.[NAME], SHIPINFO.[CITY], SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_], PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_], ITEMS.[NAME], CLCARD.[DEFINITION_]
ORDER BY satis_tutari DESC
```

## 44. P09264 — BAŞARILI

Müşteri, ürün, ödeme planı, satış temsilcisi, teslimat şehri ve birim bazında Şubat 2025 toptan net satış tutarı hesapla.

Tam sonuç: 64,438 satır. Süre: 31.32 sn. İlk durum: LIVE_PASS_SQL_PREVIEW_LIMITED. Tekrar test: Hayır.

### Üretilen SQL

```sql
SELECT UNITSETL.[NAME] AS bir, SHIPINFO.[CITY] AS teslimat_sehr, SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_] AS satis_temsilc, PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_] AS odem_pla, ITEMS.[NAME] AS urun, CLCARD.[DEFINITION_] AS muster, SUM(CASE WHEN STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (7, 8) THEN STLINE.[LINENET] ELSE -STLINE.[LINENET] END) AS toptan_net_satis_tutar
FROM [dbo].[LG_211_01_STLINE] AS STLINE
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_UNITSETL] AS UNITSETL ON STLINE.[UOMREF] = UNITSETL.[LOGICALREF]
JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_01_INVOICE] AS INVOICE ON STLINE.[INVOICEREF] = INVOICE.[LOGICALREF]

JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_ITEMS] AS ITEMS ON STLINE.[STOCKREF] = ITEMS.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_CLCARD] AS CLCARD ON INVOICE.[CLIENTREF] = CLCARD.[LOGICALREF]
WHERE STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[LINETYPE] = 0
  AND STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (3, 8)
  AND STLINE.[DATE_] >= '2025-02-01' AND STLINE.[DATE_] < '2025-03-01'
GROUP BY UNITSETL.[NAME], SHIPINFO.[CITY], SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_], PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_], ITEMS.[NAME], CLCARD.[DEFINITION_]
ORDER BY toptan_net_satis_tutar DESC
```

## 47. P09288 — BAŞARILI

```sql
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_PAYPLANS] AS PAYPLANS ON PAYPLANS.[LOGICALREF] = COALESCE(NULLIF(STLINE.[PAYDEFREF], 0), INVOICE.[PAYDEFREF])
```

## 52. P09330 — BAŞARILI

Nisan 2025 için müşteri, ürün, ödeme planı, satış temsilcisi, teslimat şehri ve birim bazında toptan net satış tutarı göster.

### Üretilen SQL

```sql
FROM [dbo].[LG_211_01_STLINE] AS STLINE
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_UNITSETL] AS UNITSETL ON STLINE.[UOMREF] = UNITSETL.[LOGICALREF]
JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_01_INVOICE] AS INVOICE ON STLINE.[INVOICEREF] = INVOICE.[LOGICALREF]

JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_ITEMS] AS ITEMS ON STLINE.[STOCKREF] = ITEMS.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_CLCARD] AS CLCARD ON INVOICE.[CLIENTREF] = CLCARD.[LOGICALREF]

  AND STLINE.[LINETYPE] = 0
  AND STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (3, 8)
  AND STLINE.[DATE_] >= '2025-04-01' AND STLINE.[DATE_] < '2025-05-01'
```

## 62. P09414 — BAŞARILI

Müşteri, ürün, ödeme planı, satış temsilcisi, teslimat şehri ve birim bazında Temmuz 2025 toptan satış tutarı hesapla.

```sql
FROM [dbo].[LG_211_01_STLINE] AS STLINE
```

### Üretilen SQL

```sql
SELECT UNITSETL.[NAME] AS bir, SHIPINFO.[CITY] AS teslimat_sehr, SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_] AS satis_temsilc, PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_] AS odem_pla, ITEMS.[NAME] AS urun, CLCARD.[DEFINITION_] AS muster, SUM(CASE WHEN STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (7, 8) THEN STLINE.[LINENET] ELSE -STLINE.[LINENET] END) AS toptan_net_satis_tutar
FROM [dbo].[LG_211_01_STLINE] AS STLINE
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_UNITSETL] AS UNITSETL ON STLINE.[UOMREF] = UNITSETL.[LOGICALREF]

JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_ITEMS] AS ITEMS ON STLINE.[STOCKREF] = ITEMS.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_CLCARD] AS CLCARD ON INVOICE.[CLIENTREF] = CLCARD.[LOGICALREF]
```

```sql
  AND STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[LINETYPE] = 0
  AND STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (3, 8)
  AND STLINE.[DATE_] >= '2025-07-01' AND STLINE.[DATE_] < '2025-08-01'
```

## 67. P09454 — BAŞARILI

Ağustos 2025 müşteri, ürün, ödeme planı, satış temsilcisi, teslimat şehri ve birim bazında toptan satış satırı sayısı nedir?

Tam sonuç: 46,627 satır. Süre: 17.33 sn. İlk durum: LIVE_PASS_SQL_PREVIEW_LIMITED. Tekrar test: Hayır.

### Üretilen SQL

```sql
SELECT UNITSETL.[NAME] AS bir, SHIPINFO.[CITY] AS teslimat_sehr, SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_] AS satis_temsilc, PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_] AS odem_pla, ITEMS.[NAME] AS urun, CLCARD.[DEFINITION_] AS muster, COUNT(STLINE.[LOGICALREF]) AS satis_satir_sayi
FROM [dbo].[LG_211_01_STLINE] AS STLINE
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_UNITSETL] AS UNITSETL ON STLINE.[UOMREF] = UNITSETL.[LOGICALREF]
JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_01_INVOICE] AS INVOICE ON STLINE.[INVOICEREF] = INVOICE.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_SHIPINFO] AS SHIPINFO ON INVOICE.[SHIPINFOREF] = SHIPINFO.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_SLSMAN] AS SLSMAN ON INVOICE.[SALESMANREF] = SLSMAN.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_PAYPLANS] AS PAYPLANS ON PAYPLANS.[LOGICALREF] = COALESCE(NULLIF(STLINE.[PAYDEFREF], 0), INVOICE.[PAYDEFREF])
JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_ITEMS] AS ITEMS ON STLINE.[STOCKREF] = ITEMS.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_CLCARD] AS CLCARD ON INVOICE.[CLIENTREF] = CLCARD.[LOGICALREF]
WHERE STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[LINETYPE] = 0
  AND STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (7, 8)
  AND STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (8)
  AND STLINE.[DATE_] >= '2025-08-01' AND STLINE.[DATE_] < '2025-09-01'
GROUP BY UNITSETL.[NAME], SHIPINFO.[CITY], SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_], PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_], ITEMS.[NAME], CLCARD.[DEFINITION_]
ORDER BY satis_satir_sayi DESC
```

## 68. P09464 — BAŞARILI

Müşteri, ürün, ödeme planı, satış temsilcisi, teslimat şehri ve birim bazında Ağustos 2025 toptan net satış tutarı hesapla.

Tam sonuç: 49,949 satır. Süre: 23.94 sn. İlk durum: LIVE_PASS_SQL_PREVIEW_LIMITED. Tekrar test: Hayır.

### Üretilen SQL

```sql
SELECT UNITSETL.[NAME] AS bir, SHIPINFO.[CITY] AS teslimat_sehr, SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_] AS satis_temsilc, PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_] AS odem_pla, ITEMS.[NAME] AS urun, CLCARD.[DEFINITION_] AS muster, SUM(CASE WHEN STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (7, 8) THEN STLINE.[LINENET] ELSE -STLINE.[LINENET] END) AS toptan_net_satis_tutar
FROM [dbo].[LG_211_01_STLINE] AS STLINE
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_UNITSETL] AS UNITSETL ON STLINE.[UOMREF] = UNITSETL.[LOGICALREF]
JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_01_INVOICE] AS INVOICE ON STLINE.[INVOICEREF] = INVOICE.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_SHIPINFO] AS SHIPINFO ON INVOICE.[SHIPINFOREF] = SHIPINFO.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_SLSMAN] AS SLSMAN ON INVOICE.[SALESMANREF] = SLSMAN.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_PAYPLANS] AS PAYPLANS ON PAYPLANS.[LOGICALREF] = COALESCE(NULLIF(STLINE.[PAYDEFREF], 0), INVOICE.[PAYDEFREF])
JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_ITEMS] AS ITEMS ON STLINE.[STOCKREF] = ITEMS.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_211_CLCARD] AS CLCARD ON INVOICE.[CLIENTREF] = CLCARD.[LOGICALREF]
WHERE STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[LINETYPE] = 0
  AND STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (3, 8)
  AND STLINE.[DATE_] >= '2025-08-01' AND STLINE.[DATE_] < '2025-09-01'
GROUP BY UNITSETL.[NAME], SHIPINFO.[CITY], SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_], PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_], ITEMS.[NAME], CLCARD.[DEFINITION_]
ORDER BY toptan_net_satis_tutar DESC
```

### Bağımsız referans SQL

```sql
JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [CANCELLED], [CLIENTREF], [DATE_], [LOGICALREF], [PAYDEFREF], [SALESMANREF], [SHIPINFOREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_01_INVOICE] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [CANCELLED], [CLIENTREF], [DATE_], [LOGICALREF], [PAYDEFREF], [SALESMANREF], [SHIPINFOREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_01_INVOICE]) AS INVOICE ON STLINE.[INVOICEREF] = INVOICE.[LOGICALREF] AND STLINE.[__nb_firm] = INVOICE.[__nb_firm]
LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [CITY], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[VW_211_SHIPINFO] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [CITY], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[VW_411_SHIPINFO]) AS SHIPINFO ON INVOICE.[SHIPINFOREF] = SHIPINFO.[LOGICALREF] AND INVOICE.[__nb_firm] = SHIPINFO.[__nb_firm]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_SLSMAN] AS SLSMAN ON INVOICE.[SALESMANREF] = SLSMAN.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_PAYPLANS] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_PAYPLANS]) AS PAYPLANS ON PAYPLANS.[LOGICALREF] = COALESCE(NULLIF(STLINE.[PAYDEFREF], 0), INVOICE.[PAYDEFREF]) AND INVOICE.[__nb_firm] = PAYPLANS.[__nb_firm]
JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [LOGICALREF], [NAME] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_ITEMS] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [LOGICALREF], [NAME] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_ITEMS]) AS ITEMS ON STLINE.[STOCKREF] = ITEMS.[LOGICALREF] AND STLINE.[__nb_firm] = ITEMS.[__nb_firm]
LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_CLCARD] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_CLCARD]) AS CLCARD ON INVOICE.[CLIENTREF] = CLCARD.[LOGICALREF] AND INVOICE.[__nb_firm] = CLCARD.[__nb_firm]
WHERE STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[LINETYPE] = 0
  AND STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (7, 8)
  AND STLINE.[DATE_] >= '2026-01-01' AND STLINE.[DATE_] < '2026-02-01'
GROUP BY UNITSETL.[NAME], SHIPINFO.[CITY], SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_], PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_], ITEMS.[NAME], CLCARD.[DEFINITION_]
ORDER BY satilan_adet DESC
```

```sql
FROM (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [CANCELLED], [DATE_], [INVOICEREF], [LINETYPE], [LOGICALREF], [PAYDEFREF], [STOCKREF], [TRCODE], [UOMREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_01_STLINE] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [CANCELLED], [DATE_], [INVOICEREF], [LINETYPE], [LOGICALREF], [PAYDEFREF], [STOCKREF], [TRCODE], [UOMREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_01_STLINE]) AS STLINE

JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [CANCELLED], [CLIENTREF], [DATE_], [LOGICALREF], [PAYDEFREF], [SALESMANREF], [SHIPINFOREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_01_INVOICE] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [CANCELLED], [CLIENTREF], [DATE_], [LOGICALREF], [PAYDEFREF], [SALESMANREF], [SHIPINFOREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_01_INVOICE]) AS INVOICE ON STLINE.[INVOICEREF] = INVOICE.[LOGICALREF] AND STLINE.[__nb_firm] = INVOICE.[__nb_firm]
LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [CITY], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[VW_211_SHIPINFO] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [CITY], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[VW_411_SHIPINFO]) AS SHIPINFO ON INVOICE.[SHIPINFOREF] = SHIPINFO.[LOGICALREF] AND INVOICE.[__nb_firm] = SHIPINFO.[__nb_firm]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_SLSMAN] AS SLSMAN ON INVOICE.[SALESMANREF] = SLSMAN.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_PAYPLANS] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_PAYPLANS]) AS PAYPLANS ON PAYPLANS.[LOGICALREF] = COALESCE(NULLIF(STLINE.[PAYDEFREF], 0), INVOICE.[PAYDEFREF]) AND INVOICE.[__nb_firm] = PAYPLANS.[__nb_firm]
JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [LOGICALREF], [NAME] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_ITEMS] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [LOGICALREF], [NAME] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_ITEMS]) AS ITEMS ON STLINE.[STOCKREF] = ITEMS.[LOGICALREF] AND STLINE.[__nb_firm] = ITEMS.[__nb_firm]
LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_CLCARD] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_CLCARD]) AS CLCARD ON INVOICE.[CLIENTREF] = CLCARD.[LOGICALREF] AND INVOICE.[__nb_firm] = CLCARD.[__nb_firm]
```

```sql
SELECT d0, d1, d2, d3, d4, d5, SUM(adet) AS adet FROM (SELECT C.DEFINITION_ AS d0, I.NAME AS d1, P.DEFINITION_ AS d2, R.DEFINITION_ AS d3, H.CITY AS d4, U.NAME AS d5, COUNT(S.LOGICALREF) AS adet FROM dbo.LG_211_01_STLINE S JOIN dbo.LG_211_ITEMS I ON S.STOCKREF=I.LOGICALREF JOIN dbo.LG_211_01_INVOICE F ON S.INVOICEREF=F.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_CLCARD C ON F.CLIENTREF=C.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_PAYPLANS P ON COALESCE(NULLIF(S.PAYDEFREF,0),F.PAYDEFREF)=P.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_SLSMAN R ON F.SALESMANREF=R.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_SHIPINFO H ON F.SHIPINFOREF=H.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_UNITSETL U ON S.UOMREF=U.LOGICALREF WHERE S.CANCELLED=0 AND S.LINETYPE=0 AND S.TRCODE IN (7,8) AND S.DATE_ >= '2026-01-01' AND S.DATE_ < '2026-02-01' GROUP BY C.DEFINITION_, I.NAME, P.DEFINITION_, R.DEFINITION_, H.CITY, U.NAME UNION ALL SELECT C.DEFINITION_ AS d0, I.NAME AS d1, P.DEFINITION_ AS d2, R.DEFINITION_ AS d3, H.CITY AS d4, U.NAME AS d5, COUNT(S.LOGICALREF) AS adet FROM dbo.LG_411_01_STLINE S JOIN dbo.LG_411_ITEMS I ON S.STOCKREF=I.LOGICALREF JOIN dbo.LG_411_01_INVOICE F ON S.INVOICEREF=F.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_CLCARD C ON F.CLIENTREF=C.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_PAYPLANS P ON COALESCE(NULLIF(S.PAYDEFREF,0),F.PAYDEFREF)=P.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_SLSMAN R ON F.SALESMANREF=R.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_SHIPINFO H ON F.SHIPINFOREF=H.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_UNITSETL U ON S.UOMREF=U.LOGICALREF WHERE S.CANCELLED=0 AND S.LINETYPE=0 AND S.TRCODE IN (7,8) AND S.DATE_ >= '2026-01-01' AND S.DATE_ < '2026-02-01' GROUP BY C.DEFINITION_, I.NAME, P.DEFINITION_, R.DEFINITION_, H.CITY, U.NAME) AS firms GROUP BY d0, d1, d2, d3, d4, d5
```

```sql
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_SLSMAN] AS SLSMAN ON INVOICE.[SALESMANREF] = SLSMAN.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_PAYPLANS] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_PAYPLANS]) AS PAYPLANS ON PAYPLANS.[LOGICALREF] = COALESCE(NULLIF(STLINE.[PAYDEFREF], 0), INVOICE.[PAYDEFREF]) AND INVOICE.[__nb_firm] = PAYPLANS.[__nb_firm]

LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_PAYPLANS] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_PAYPLANS]) AS PAYPLANS ON PAYPLANS.[LOGICALREF] = COALESCE(NULLIF(STLINE.[PAYDEFREF], 0), INVOICE.[PAYDEFREF]) AND INVOICE.[__nb_firm] = PAYPLANS.[__nb_firm]
```
```sql
JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [LOGICALREF], [NAME] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_ITEMS] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [LOGICALREF], [NAME] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_ITEMS]) AS ITEMS ON STLINE.[STOCKREF] = ITEMS.[LOGICALREF] AND STLINE.[__nb_firm] = ITEMS.[__nb_firm]
LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_CLCARD] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_CLCARD]) AS CLCARD ON INVOICE.[CLIENTREF] = CLCARD.[LOGICALREF] AND INVOICE.[__nb_firm] = CLCARD.[__nb_firm]
WHERE STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0

GROUP BY UNITSETL.[NAME], SHIPINFO.[CITY], SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_], PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_], ITEMS.[NAME], CLCARD.[DEFINITION_]
```
```sql
ORDER BY satis_tutar DESC
```

### Üretilen SQL

```sql
SELECT UNITSETL.[NAME] AS bir, SHIPINFO.[CITY] AS teslimat_sehr, SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_] AS satis_temsilc, PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_] AS odem_pla, ITEMS.[NAME] AS urun, CLCARD.[DEFINITION_] AS muster, SUM(STLINE.[LINENET]) AS satis_tutar
FROM (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [CANCELLED], [DATE_], [INVOICEREF], [LINENET], [LINETYPE], [PAYDEFREF], [STOCKREF], [TRCODE], [UOMREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_01_STLINE] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [CANCELLED], [DATE_], [INVOICEREF], [LINENET], [LINETYPE], [PAYDEFREF], [STOCKREF], [TRCODE], [UOMREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_01_STLINE]) AS STLINE
LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [LOGICALREF], [NAME] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_UNITSETL] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [LOGICALREF], [NAME] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_UNITSETL]) AS UNITSETL ON STLINE.[UOMREF] = UNITSETL.[LOGICALREF] AND STLINE.[__nb_firm] = UNITSETL.[__nb_firm]
JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [CANCELLED], [CLIENTREF], [DATE_], [LOGICALREF], [PAYDEFREF], [SALESMANREF], [SHIPINFOREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_01_INVOICE] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [CANCELLED], [CLIENTREF], [DATE_], [LOGICALREF], [PAYDEFREF], [SALESMANREF], [SHIPINFOREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_01_INVOICE]) AS INVOICE ON STLINE.[INVOICEREF] = INVOICE.[LOGICALREF] AND STLINE.[__nb_firm] = INVOICE.[__nb_firm]
LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [CITY], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[VW_211_SHIPINFO] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [CITY], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[VW_411_SHIPINFO]) AS SHIPINFO ON INVOICE.[SHIPINFOREF] = SHIPINFO.[LOGICALREF] AND INVOICE.[__nb_firm] = SHIPINFO.[__nb_firm]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_SLSMAN] AS SLSMAN ON INVOICE.[SALESMANREF] = SLSMAN.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_PAYPLANS] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_PAYPLANS]) AS PAYPLANS ON PAYPLANS.[LOGICALREF] = COALESCE(NULLIF(STLINE.[PAYDEFREF], 0), INVOICE.[PAYDEFREF]) AND INVOICE.[__nb_firm] = PAYPLANS.[__nb_firm]
JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [LOGICALREF], [NAME] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_ITEMS] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [LOGICALREF], [NAME] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_ITEMS]) AS ITEMS ON STLINE.[STOCKREF] = ITEMS.[LOGICALREF] AND STLINE.[__nb_firm] = ITEMS.[__nb_firm]
LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_CLCARD] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_CLCARD]) AS CLCARD ON INVOICE.[CLIENTREF] = CLCARD.[LOGICALREF] AND INVOICE.[__nb_firm] = CLCARD.[__nb_firm]
WHERE STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[LINETYPE] = 0
  AND STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (7, 8)
  AND STLINE.[DATE_] >= '2026-04-01' AND STLINE.[DATE_] < '2026-05-01'
GROUP BY UNITSETL.[NAME], SHIPINFO.[CITY], SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_], PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_], ITEMS.[NAME], CLCARD.[DEFINITION_]
ORDER BY satis_tutar DESC
```

### Bağımsız referans SQL

```sql
SELECT d0, d1, d2, d3, d4, d5, SUM(adet) AS adet FROM (SELECT C.DEFINITION_ AS d0, I.NAME AS d1, P.DEFINITION_ AS d2, R.DEFINITION_ AS d3, H.CITY AS d4, U.NAME AS d5, SUM(S.LINENET) AS adet FROM dbo.LG_211_01_STLINE S JOIN dbo.LG_211_ITEMS I ON S.STOCKREF=I.LOGICALREF JOIN dbo.LG_211_01_INVOICE F ON S.INVOICEREF=F.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_CLCARD C ON F.CLIENTREF=C.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_PAYPLANS P ON COALESCE(NULLIF(S.PAYDEFREF,0),F.PAYDEFREF)=P.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_SLSMAN R ON F.SALESMANREF=R.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_SHIPINFO H ON F.SHIPINFOREF=H.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_UNITSETL U ON S.UOMREF=U.LOGICALREF WHERE S.CANCELLED=0 AND S.LINETYPE=0 AND S.TRCODE IN (7,8) AND S.DATE_ >= '2026-04-01' AND S.DATE_ < '2026-05-01' GROUP BY C.DEFINITION_, I.NAME, P.DEFINITION_, R.DEFINITION_, H.CITY, U.NAME UNION ALL SELECT C.DEFINITION_ AS d0, I.NAME AS d1, P.DEFINITION_ AS d2, R.DEFINITION_ AS d3, H.CITY AS d4, U.NAME AS d5, SUM(S.LINENET) AS adet FROM dbo.LG_411_01_STLINE S JOIN dbo.LG_411_ITEMS I ON S.STOCKREF=I.LOGICALREF JOIN dbo.LG_411_01_INVOICE F ON S.INVOICEREF=F.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_CLCARD C ON F.CLIENTREF=C.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_PAYPLANS P ON COALESCE(NULLIF(S.PAYDEFREF,0),F.PAYDEFREF)=P.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_SLSMAN R ON F.SALESMANREF=R.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_SHIPINFO H ON F.SHIPINFOREF=H.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_UNITSETL U ON S.UOMREF=U.LOGICALREF WHERE S.CANCELLED=0 AND S.LINETYPE=0 AND S.TRCODE IN (7,8) AND S.DATE_ >= '2026-04-01' AND S.DATE_ < '2026-05-01' GROUP BY C.DEFINITION_, I.NAME, P.DEFINITION_, R.DEFINITION_, H.CITY, U.NAME) AS firms GROUP BY d0, d1, d2, d3, d4, d5
```

```sql
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_SLSMAN] AS SLSMAN ON INVOICE.[SALESMANREF] = SLSMAN.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_PAYPLANS] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_PAYPLANS]) AS PAYPLANS ON PAYPLANS.[LOGICALREF] = COALESCE(NULLIF(STLINE.[PAYDEFREF], 0), INVOICE.[PAYDEFREF]) AND INVOICE.[__nb_firm] = PAYPLANS.[__nb_firm]
JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [LOGICALREF], [NAME] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_ITEMS] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [LOGICALREF], [NAME] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_ITEMS]) AS ITEMS ON STLINE.[STOCKREF] = ITEMS.[LOGICALREF] AND STLINE.[__nb_firm] = ITEMS.[__nb_firm]
LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_CLCARD] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_CLCARD]) AS CLCARD ON INVOICE.[CLIENTREF] = CLCARD.[LOGICALREF] AND INVOICE.[__nb_firm] = CLCARD.[__nb_firm]
WHERE STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[LINETYPE] = 0
  AND STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (2, 3, 7, 8)
  AND STLINE.[DATE_] >= '2026-04-01' AND STLINE.[DATE_] < '2026-05-01'
GROUP BY UNITSETL.[NAME], SHIPINFO.[CITY], SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_], PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_], ITEMS.[NAME], CLCARD.[DEFINITION_]
```

### Bağımsız referans SQL

```sql
SELECT d0, d1, d2, d3, d4, d5, SUM(adet) AS adet FROM (SELECT C.DEFINITION_ AS d0, I.NAME AS d1, P.DEFINITION_ AS d2, R.DEFINITION_ AS d3, H.CITY AS d4, U.NAME AS d5, SUM(CASE WHEN S.TRCODE IN (2,3) THEN -S.LINENET ELSE S.LINENET END) AS adet FROM dbo.LG_211_01_STLINE S JOIN dbo.LG_211_ITEMS I ON S.STOCKREF=I.LOGICALREF JOIN dbo.LG_211_01_INVOICE F ON S.INVOICEREF=F.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_CLCARD C ON F.CLIENTREF=C.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_PAYPLANS P ON COALESCE(NULLIF(S.PAYDEFREF,0),F.PAYDEFREF)=P.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_SLSMAN R ON F.SALESMANREF=R.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_SHIPINFO H ON F.SHIPINFOREF=H.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_UNITSETL U ON S.UOMREF=U.LOGICALREF WHERE S.CANCELLED=0 AND S.LINETYPE=0 AND S.TRCODE IN (2,3,7,8) AND S.DATE_ >= '2026-04-01' AND S.DATE_ < '2026-05-01' GROUP BY C.DEFINITION_, I.NAME, P.DEFINITION_, R.DEFINITION_, H.CITY, U.NAME UNION ALL SELECT C.DEFINITION_ AS d0, I.NAME AS d1, P.DEFINITION_ AS d2, R.DEFINITION_ AS d3, H.CITY AS d4, U.NAME AS d5, SUM(CASE WHEN S.TRCODE IN (2,3) THEN -S.LINENET ELSE S.LINENET END) AS adet FROM dbo.LG_411_01_STLINE S JOIN dbo.LG_411_ITEMS I ON S.STOCKREF=I.LOGICALREF JOIN dbo.LG_411_01_INVOICE F ON S.INVOICEREF=F.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_CLCARD C ON F.CLIENTREF=C.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_PAYPLANS P ON COALESCE(NULLIF(S.PAYDEFREF,0),F.PAYDEFREF)=P.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_SLSMAN R ON F.SALESMANREF=R.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_SHIPINFO H ON F.SHIPINFOREF=H.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_UNITSETL U ON S.UOMREF=U.LOGICALREF WHERE S.CANCELLED=0 AND S.LINETYPE=0 AND S.TRCODE IN (2,3,7,8) AND S.DATE_ >= '2026-04-01' AND S.DATE_ < '2026-05-01' GROUP BY C.DEFINITION_, I.NAME, P.DEFINITION_, R.DEFINITION_, H.CITY, U.NAME) AS firms GROUP BY d0, d1, d2, d3, d4, d5
```

```sql
LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_PAYPLANS] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_PAYPLANS]) AS PAYPLANS ON PAYPLANS.[LOGICALREF] = COALESCE(NULLIF(STLINE.[PAYDEFREF], 0), INVOICE.[PAYDEFREF]) AND INVOICE.[__nb_firm] = PAYPLANS.[__nb_firm]

LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_PAYPLANS] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_PAYPLANS]) AS PAYPLANS ON PAYPLANS.[LOGICALREF] = COALESCE(NULLIF(STLINE.[PAYDEFREF], 0), INVOICE.[PAYDEFREF]) AND INVOICE.[__nb_firm] = PAYPLANS.[__nb_firm]

GROUP BY UNITSETL.[NAME], SHIPINFO.[CITY], SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_], PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_], ITEMS.[NAME], CLCARD.[DEFINITION_]
ORDER BY satilan_adet DESC
```

```sql
FROM (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [CANCELLED], [DATE_], [INVOICEREF], [LINENET], [LINETYPE], [PAYDEFREF], [STOCKREF], [TRCODE], [UOMREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_01_STLINE] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [CANCELLED], [DATE_], [INVOICEREF], [LINENET], [LINETYPE], [PAYDEFREF], [STOCKREF], [TRCODE], [UOMREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_01_STLINE]) AS STLINE

LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_SLSMAN] AS SLSMAN ON INVOICE.[SALESMANREF] = SLSMAN.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_PAYPLANS] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_PAYPLANS]) AS PAYPLANS ON PAYPLANS.[LOGICALREF] = COALESCE(NULLIF(STLINE.[PAYDEFREF], 0), INVOICE.[PAYDEFREF]) AND INVOICE.[__nb_firm] = PAYPLANS.[__nb_firm]

WHERE STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0

  AND STLINE.[LINETYPE] = 0
```

### Üretilen SQL

```sql
SELECT UNITSETL.[NAME] AS bir, SHIPINFO.[CITY] AS teslimat_sehr, SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_] AS satis_temsilc, PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_] AS odem_pla, ITEMS.[NAME] AS urun, CLCARD.[DEFINITION_] AS muster, SUM(STLINE.[AMOUNT]) AS satilan_adet
```
```sql
FROM (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [AMOUNT], [CANCELLED], [DATE_], [INVOICEREF], [LINETYPE], [PAYDEFREF], [STOCKREF], [TRCODE], [UOMREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_01_STLINE] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [AMOUNT], [CANCELLED], [DATE_], [INVOICEREF], [LINETYPE], [PAYDEFREF], [STOCKREF], [TRCODE], [UOMREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_01_STLINE]) AS STLINE
LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [LOGICALREF], [NAME] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_UNITSETL] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [LOGICALREF], [NAME] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_UNITSETL]) AS UNITSETL ON STLINE.[UOMREF] = UNITSETL.[LOGICALREF] AND STLINE.[__nb_firm] = UNITSETL.[__nb_firm]
JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [CANCELLED], [CLIENTREF], [DATE_], [LOGICALREF], [PAYDEFREF], [SALESMANREF], [SHIPINFOREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_01_INVOICE] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [CANCELLED], [CLIENTREF], [DATE_], [LOGICALREF], [PAYDEFREF], [SALESMANREF], [SHIPINFOREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_01_INVOICE]) AS INVOICE ON STLINE.[INVOICEREF] = INVOICE.[LOGICALREF] AND STLINE.[__nb_firm] = INVOICE.[__nb_firm]
LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [CITY], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[VW_211_SHIPINFO] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [CITY], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[VW_411_SHIPINFO]) AS SHIPINFO ON INVOICE.[SHIPINFOREF] = SHIPINFO.[LOGICALREF] AND INVOICE.[__nb_firm] = SHIPINFO.[__nb_firm]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_SLSMAN] AS SLSMAN ON INVOICE.[SALESMANREF] = SLSMAN.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_PAYPLANS] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_PAYPLANS]) AS PAYPLANS ON PAYPLANS.[LOGICALREF] = COALESCE(NULLIF(STLINE.[PAYDEFREF], 0), INVOICE.[PAYDEFREF]) AND INVOICE.[__nb_firm] = PAYPLANS.[__nb_firm]
JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [LOGICALREF], [NAME] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_ITEMS] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [LOGICALREF], [NAME] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_ITEMS]) AS ITEMS ON STLINE.[STOCKREF] = ITEMS.[LOGICALREF] AND STLINE.[__nb_firm] = ITEMS.[__nb_firm]
LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_CLCARD] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_CLCARD]) AS CLCARD ON INVOICE.[CLIENTREF] = CLCARD.[LOGICALREF] AND INVOICE.[__nb_firm] = CLCARD.[__nb_firm]
WHERE STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[LINETYPE] = 0
  AND STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (7, 8)
```
```sql
  AND STLINE.[DATE_] >= '2026-07-01' AND STLINE.[DATE_] < '2026-08-01'
GROUP BY UNITSETL.[NAME], SHIPINFO.[CITY], SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_], PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_], ITEMS.[NAME], CLCARD.[DEFINITION_]
ORDER BY satilan_adet DESC
```

```sql
SELECT d0, d1, d2, d3, d4, d5, SUM(adet) AS adet FROM (SELECT C.DEFINITION_ AS d0, I.NAME AS d1, P.DEFINITION_ AS d2, R.DEFINITION_ AS d3, H.CITY AS d4, U.NAME AS d5, SUM(CASE WHEN S.TRCODE IN (2,3) THEN -S.LINENET ELSE S.LINENET END) AS adet FROM dbo.LG_211_01_STLINE S JOIN dbo.LG_211_ITEMS I ON S.STOCKREF=I.LOGICALREF JOIN dbo.LG_211_01_INVOICE F ON S.INVOICEREF=F.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_CLCARD C ON F.CLIENTREF=C.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_PAYPLANS P ON COALESCE(NULLIF(S.PAYDEFREF,0),F.PAYDEFREF)=P.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_SLSMAN R ON F.SALESMANREF=R.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_SHIPINFO H ON F.SHIPINFOREF=H.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_UNITSETL U ON S.UOMREF=U.LOGICALREF WHERE S.CANCELLED=0 AND S.LINETYPE=0 AND S.TRCODE IN (2,3,7,8) AND S.DATE_ >= '2026-07-01' AND S.DATE_ < '2026-08-01' GROUP BY C.DEFINITION_, I.NAME, P.DEFINITION_, R.DEFINITION_, H.CITY, U.NAME UNION ALL SELECT C.DEFINITION_ AS d0, I.NAME AS d1, P.DEFINITION_ AS d2, R.DEFINITION_ AS d3, H.CITY AS d4, U.NAME AS d5, SUM(CASE WHEN S.TRCODE IN (2,3) THEN -S.LINENET ELSE S.LINENET END) AS adet FROM dbo.LG_411_01_STLINE S JOIN dbo.LG_411_ITEMS I ON S.STOCKREF=I.LOGICALREF JOIN dbo.LG_411_01_INVOICE F ON S.INVOICEREF=F.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_CLCARD C ON F.CLIENTREF=C.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_PAYPLANS P ON COALESCE(NULLIF(S.PAYDEFREF,0),F.PAYDEFREF)=P.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_SLSMAN R ON F.SALESMANREF=R.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_SHIPINFO H ON F.SHIPINFOREF=H.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_UNITSETL U ON S.UOMREF=U.LOGICALREF WHERE S.CANCELLED=0 AND S.LINETYPE=0 AND S.TRCODE IN (2,3,7,8) AND S.DATE_ >= '2026-07-01' AND S.DATE_ < '2026-08-01' GROUP BY C.DEFINITION_, I.NAME, P.DEFINITION_, R.DEFINITION_, H.CITY, U.NAME) AS firms GROUP BY d0, d1, d2, d3, d4, d5
```

```sql
JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [CANCELLED], [CLIENTREF], [DATE_], [LOGICALREF], [PAYDEFREF], [SALESMANREF], [SHIPINFOREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_01_INVOICE] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [CANCELLED], [CLIENTREF], [DATE_], [LOGICALREF], [PAYDEFREF], [SALESMANREF], [SHIPINFOREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_01_INVOICE]) AS INVOICE ON STLINE.[INVOICEREF] = INVOICE.[LOGICALREF] AND STLINE.[__nb_firm] = INVOICE.[__nb_firm]

LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_SLSMAN] AS SLSMAN ON INVOICE.[SALESMANREF] = SLSMAN.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_PAYPLANS] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_PAYPLANS]) AS PAYPLANS ON PAYPLANS.[LOGICALREF] = COALESCE(NULLIF(STLINE.[PAYDEFREF], 0), INVOICE.[PAYDEFREF]) AND INVOICE.[__nb_firm] = PAYPLANS.[__nb_firm]
```

```sql
LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [CITY], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[VW_211_SHIPINFO] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [CITY], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[VW_411_SHIPINFO]) AS SHIPINFO ON INVOICE.[SHIPINFOREF] = SHIPINFO.[LOGICALREF] AND INVOICE.[__nb_firm] = SHIPINFO.[__nb_firm]
LEFT JOIN [dbo].[LG_SLSMAN] AS SLSMAN ON INVOICE.[SALESMANREF] = SLSMAN.[LOGICALREF]
LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_PAYPLANS] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_PAYPLANS]) AS PAYPLANS ON PAYPLANS.[LOGICALREF] = COALESCE(NULLIF(STLINE.[PAYDEFREF], 0), INVOICE.[PAYDEFREF]) AND INVOICE.[__nb_firm] = PAYPLANS.[__nb_firm]
JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [LOGICALREF], [NAME] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_ITEMS] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [LOGICALREF], [NAME] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_ITEMS]) AS ITEMS ON STLINE.[STOCKREF] = ITEMS.[LOGICALREF] AND STLINE.[__nb_firm] = ITEMS.[__nb_firm]
LEFT JOIN (SELECT '211' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_211_CLCARD] UNION ALL SELECT '411' AS [__nb_firm], [DEFINITION_], [LOGICALREF] FROM [dbo].[LG_411_CLCARD]) AS CLCARD ON INVOICE.[CLIENTREF] = CLCARD.[LOGICALREF] AND INVOICE.[__nb_firm] = CLCARD.[__nb_firm]
WHERE STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[CANCELLED] = 0
  AND STLINE.[LINETYPE] = 0
  AND STLINE.[TRCODE] IN (2, 3, 7, 8)
  AND STLINE.[DATE_] >= '2026-08-01' AND STLINE.[DATE_] < '2026-09-01'
GROUP BY UNITSETL.[NAME], SHIPINFO.[CITY], SLSMAN.[DEFINITION_], PAYPLANS.[DEFINITION_], ITEMS.[NAME], CLCARD.[DEFINITION_]
ORDER BY net_satis_tutar DESC
```

### Bağımsız referans SQL

```sql
SELECT d0, d1, d2, d3, d4, d5, SUM(adet) AS adet FROM (SELECT C.DEFINITION_ AS d0, I.NAME AS d1, P.DEFINITION_ AS d2, R.DEFINITION_ AS d3, H.CITY AS d4, U.NAME AS d5, SUM(CASE WHEN S.TRCODE IN (2,3) THEN -S.LINENET ELSE S.LINENET END) AS adet FROM dbo.LG_211_01_STLINE S JOIN dbo.LG_211_ITEMS I ON S.STOCKREF=I.LOGICALREF JOIN dbo.LG_211_01_INVOICE F ON S.INVOICEREF=F.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_CLCARD C ON F.CLIENTREF=C.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_PAYPLANS P ON COALESCE(NULLIF(S.PAYDEFREF,0),F.PAYDEFREF)=P.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_SLSMAN R ON F.SALESMANREF=R.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_SHIPINFO H ON F.SHIPINFOREF=H.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_211_UNITSETL U ON S.UOMREF=U.LOGICALREF WHERE S.CANCELLED=0 AND S.LINETYPE=0 AND S.TRCODE IN (2,3,7,8) AND S.DATE_ >= '2026-08-01' AND S.DATE_ < '2026-09-01' GROUP BY C.DEFINITION_, I.NAME, P.DEFINITION_, R.DEFINITION_, H.CITY, U.NAME UNION ALL SELECT C.DEFINITION_ AS d0, I.NAME AS d1, P.DEFINITION_ AS d2, R.DEFINITION_ AS d3, H.CITY AS d4, U.NAME AS d5, SUM(CASE WHEN S.TRCODE IN (2,3) THEN -S.LINENET ELSE S.LINENET END) AS adet FROM dbo.LG_411_01_STLINE S JOIN dbo.LG_411_ITEMS I ON S.STOCKREF=I.LOGICALREF JOIN dbo.LG_411_01_INVOICE F ON S.INVOICEREF=F.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_CLCARD C ON F.CLIENTREF=C.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_PAYPLANS P ON COALESCE(NULLIF(S.PAYDEFREF,0),F.PAYDEFREF)=P.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_SLSMAN R ON F.SALESMANREF=R.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_SHIPINFO H ON F.SHIPINFOREF=H.LOGICALREF LEFT JOIN dbo.LG_411_UNITSETL U ON S.UOMREF=U.LOGICALREF WHERE S.CANCELLED=0 AND S.LINETYPE=0 AND S.TRCODE IN (2,3,7,8) AND S.DATE_ >= '2026-08-01' AND S.DATE_ < '2026-09-01' GROUP BY C.DEFINITION_, I.NAME, P.DEFINITION_, R.DEFINITION_, H.CITY, U.NAME) AS firms GROUP BY d0, d1, d2, d3, d4, d5
```
